Fuzzy String Matching\n\n## Overview\nFuzzy string matching finds approximate matches between strings, useful for searching company names, addresses, or any text data where exact matches may not exist due to variations in spelling, formatting, or abbreviations.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\n# Install fuzzywuzzy library\npip install fuzzywuzzy\n\n# Install python-Levenshtein for better performance (optional but recommended)\npip install python-Levenshtein\n\n# Alternative: rapidfuzz (faster, drop-in replacement)\npip install rapidfuzz\n```\n\n## Basic Usage with fuzzywuzzy\n\n### Simple Ratio\n```python\nfrom fuzzywuzzy import fuzz\n\n# Simple ratio - basic string similarity\nscore = fuzz.ratio(\"RENAISSANCE TECHNOLOGIES\", \"Renaissance Technologies LLC\")\nprint(score)  # Returns 0-100, higher is better match\n```\n\n### Partial Ratio\n```python\n# Partial ratio - finds best matching substring\nscore = fuzz.partial_ratio(\"RENAISSANCE TECHNOLOGIES\", \"Renaissance Technologies LLC\")\n# Better for when one string is a substring of another\n```\n\n### Token Sort Ratio\n```python\n# Token sort ratio - sorts tokens alphabetically before comparing\nscore = fuzz.token_sort_ratio(\"Technologies Renaissance\", \"Renaissance Technologies\")\n# Useful when word order may vary\n```\n\n### Token Set Ratio\n```python\n# Token set ratio - compares sets of tokens\nscore = fuzz.token_set_ratio(\n    \"Renaissance Technologies LLC\",\n    \"Renaissance Technologies Corporation\"\n)\n# Best for handling extra words or abbreviations\n```\n\n## Finding Best Matches with process\n\n### Extract One - Single Best Match\n```python\nfrom fuzzywuzzy import process\n\n# List of choices to search\ncompanies = [\n    \"Apple Inc\",\n    \"Microsoft Corporation\",\n    \"Renaissance Technologies LLC\",\n    \"Berkshire Hathaway Inc\"\n]\n\n# Find single best match\nsearch_term = \"renaissance technologies\"\nbest_match = process.extractOne(search_term, companies)\nprint(best_match)\n# Returns: ('Renaissance Technologies LLC', 95)\n# Tuple of (match, score)\n\n# With score threshold\nbest_match = process.extractOne(search_term, companies, score_cutoff=80)\n# Returns None if no match above threshold\n```\n\n### Extract - Multiple Matches\n```python\n# Get top N matches\ntop_matches = process.extract(\"renaissance\", companies, limit=3)\nprint(top_matches)\n# Returns: [('Renaissance Technologies LLC', 95), ...]\n# List of (match, score) tuples sorted by score\n```\n\n### Using Custom Scorers\n```python\n# Use specific scoring function\nbest_match = process.extractOne(\n    \"warren buffett berkshire\",\n    companies,\n    scorer=fuzz.token_set_ratio\n)\n```\n\n## Working with Pandas DataFrames\n\n### Find Matching Rows\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom fuzzywuzzy import process\n\ndef find_best_match_in_dataframe(df, column_name, search_term, threshold=80):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find best matching row in a DataFrame column\n\n    Args:\n        df: pandas DataFrame\n        column_name: name of column to search\n        search_term: string to search for\n        threshold: minimum score (0-100) to consider a match\n\n    Returns:\n        DataFrame row with best match, or None\n    \"\"\"\n    # Get all values from the column\n    choices = df[column_name].dropna().tolist()\n\n    # Find best match\n    result = process.extractOne(search_term, choices, score_cutoff=threshold)\n\n    if result:\n        matched_value, score = result\n        # Return the row(s) that match\n        matched_rows = df[df[column_name] == matched_value]\n        return matched_rows.iloc[0] if len(matched_rows) > 0 else None\n\n    return None\n\n# Usage\ndf = pd.read_csv('companies.tsv', sep='\\t')\nmatch = find_best_match_in_dataframe(\n    df,\n    'FILINGMANAGER_NAME',\n    'renaissance technologies',\n    threshold=70\n)\nif match is not None:\n    print(f\"Found: {match['FILINGMANAGER_NAME']}\")\n```\n\n### Get All Good Matches\n```python\ndef find_all_matches(df, column_name, search_term, threshold=80, limit=5):\n    \"\"\"Find multiple good matches\"\"\"\n    choices = df[column_name].dropna().tolist()\n    results = process.extract(search_term, choices, limit=limit)\n\n    matches = []\n    for matched_value, score in results:\n        if score >= threshold:\n            matched_rows = df[df[column_name] == matched_value]\n            matches.append((matched_rows.iloc[0], score))\n\n    return matches\n```\n\n## Using RapidFuzz (Faster Alternative)\n\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz, process\n\n# Same API as fuzzywuzzy but faster\nscore = fuzz.ratio(\"string1\", \"string2\")\n\n# Process functions\nbest = find_fund_accession(coverpage_df, fund_name, threshold=80):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find accession number for a fund by fuzzy matching name\n\n    Args:\n        coverpage_df: DataFrame from COVERPAGE.tsv\n        fund_name: name to search for (e.g., \"berkshire hathaway\")\n        threshold: minimum match score\n\n    Returns:\n        accession_number or None\n    \"\"\"\n    from fuzzywuzzy import process\n\n    # Get all fund names\n    fund_names = coverpage_df['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].dropna().unique().tolist()\n\n    # Find best match\n    result = process.extractOne(fund_name, fund_names, score_cutoff=threshold)\n\n    if result:\n        matched_name, score = result\n        print(f\"Matched '{fund_name}' to '{matched_name}' (score: {score})\")\n\n        # Get accession number for this fund\n        match_row = coverpage_df[\n            coverpage_df['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'] == matched_name\n        ]\n\n        if not match_row.empty:\n            return match_row.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n\n    return None\n```\n\n### Search for Company by Name to Get CUSIP\n```python\ndef find_cusip_by_company_name(infotable_df, company_name, threshold=80):\n threshold: minimum match score\n\n    Returns:\n        CUSIP or None\n    \"\"\"\n    from fuzzywuzzy import process\n\n    # Get unique company names\n    companies = infotable_df['NAMEOFISSUER'].dropna().unique().tolist()\n\n    # Find best match\n    result = process.extractOne(company_name, companies, score_cutoff=threshold)\n\n    if result:\n        matched_name, score = result\n        print(f\"Matched '{company_name}' to '{matched_name}' (score: {score})\")\n\n        # Get CUSIP for this company\n        match_row = infotable_df[\n            infotable_df['NAMEOFISSUER'] == matched_name\n        ]\n\n        if not match_row.empty:\n            return match_row.iloc[0]['CUSIP']\n\n    return None\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Choose the right scorer**:\n   - `ratio`: Basic similarity, exact order matters\n   - `partial_ratio`: One string contains the other\n   - `token_sort_ratio`: Word order doesn't matter\n   - `token_set_ratio`: Extra words or abbreviations present\n\n2. **Set appropriate thresholds**:\n   - 90+: Very strict, almost exact matches\n   - 80-89: Good matches with minor differences\n   - 70-79: Moderate similarity, check results\n   - <70: May include false positives\n\n3. **Normalize strings before matching**:\n   ```python\n   def normalize(s):\n       return s.lower().strip()\n\n - Reduces computation and avoids duplicate results\n\n5. **Performance optimization**:\n   - Use `rapidfuzz` instead of `fuzzywuzzy` for large datasets\n   - Limit search space by pre-filtering when `token_set_ratio`: Extra words or abbreviations present\n\n2. **Set appropriate thresholds**:\n   - 90+: Very strict, almost exact matches\n   - 80-89: Good matches with minor differences\n   - 70-79: search_term = normalize(\"Renaissance Technologies\")\n   choices = [normalize(c) for c in choices]\n   ```\n\n4. **Handle duplicates**:\n   - Use `.unique()` on DataFrame columns before matching\n   - Reduces computation and avoids duplicate results\n\n5. **Performance optimization**:\n   - Use `rapidfuzz` instead of `fuzzywuzzy` for large datasets\n   - Limit search space by pre-filtering when possible\n   - Cache results if searching repeatedly\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

It discloses their equity holdings, providing transparency into the investment strategies of major hedge funds, pension funds, and other institutional investors.\n\n## Data Files\n\nThe SEC provides Form 13F data in TSV (Tab-Separated Values) format across multiple files:\n\n### 1. SUBMISSION.tsv\nBasic submission metadata\n- **ACCESSION_NUMBER**: Unique identifier for each filing (primary key)\n- **FILING_DATE**: When the form was filed\n- **SUBMISSIONTYPE**: Type (13F-HR, 13F-HR/A for amendments)\n- **CIK**: Central Index Key for the filer\n- **PERIODOFREPORT**: Quarter end date\n\n### 2. COVERPAGE.tsv\nCover page information about the filing manager\n- **ACCESSION_NUMBER**: Links to submission (primary key)\n- **REPORTCALENDARORQUARTER**: Quarter end date\n- **FILINGMANAGER_NAME**: Name of the fund/manager (e.g., \"Berkshire Hathaway Inc\")\n- **FILINGMANAGER_STREET1/2**: Address\n- **FILINGMANAGER_CITY/STATEORCOUNTRY/ZIPCODE**: Location\n- **REPORTTYPE**: Usually \"13F HOLDINGS REPORT\"\n- **FORM13FFILENUMBER**: SEC file number\n- **ISAMENDMENT**: Y/N if this is an amendment\n\n### 3. SUMMARYPAGE.tsv\nSummary statistics for the filing\n- **ACCESSION_NUMBER**: Links to submission (primary key)\n- **OTHERINCLUDEDMANAGERSCOUNT**: Number of other managers\n- **TABLEENTRYTOTAL**: Total number of holdings entries\n- **TABLEVALUETOTAL**: **Total AUM in dollars** (key metric!)\n- **ISCONFIDENTIALOMITTED**: If confidential info was omitted\n\n### 4. INFOTABLE.tsv\nDetailed holdings information (the main data table)\n- **ACCESSION_NUMBER**: Links to submission\n- **INFOTABLE_SK**: Surrogate key for this entry\n- **NAMEOFISSUER**: Company name (e.g., \"APPLE INC\")\n- **TITLEOFCLASS**: Security type (usually \"COM\" for common stock)\n- **CUSIP**: 9-digit security identifier (e.g., \"037833100\" for Apple)\n- **VALUE**: Market value in dollars (starting Jan 2023, exact dollars; before that, thousands)\n- **SSHPRNAMT**: Number of shares or principal amount\n- **SSHPRNAMTTYPE**: \"SH\" for shares, \"PRN\" for principal\n- **PUTCALL**: If it's a put or call option\n- **INVESTMENTDISCRETION**: Who has discretion (SOLE, SHARED, DFND)\n- **VOTING_AUTH_SOLE**: Shares with sole voting authority\n- **VOTING_AUTH_SHARED**: Shares with shared voting authority\n- **VOTING_AUTH_NONE**: Shares with no voting authority\n\n### 5. SIGNATURE.tsv\nSignature information\n- **ACCESSION_NUMBER**: Links to submission\n- **NAME**: Signatory name\n- **TITLE**: Title of signatory\n- **SIGNATUREDATE**: Date signed\n\n### 6. OTHERMANAGER.tsv & OTHERMANAGER2.tsv\nInformation about other managers included in the report\n\n## Key Relationships\n\n```\nSUBMISSION ─┬─ COVERPAGE (1:1)\n            ├─ SUMMARYPAGE (1:1)\n            ├─ INFOTABLE (1:many)\n            ├─ SIGNATURE (1:1)\n            └─ OTHERMANAGER (1:many)\n\nPrimary Key: ACCESSION_NUMBER\n```\n\n## Common Analysis Patterns\n\n### Find a Fund's Accession Number\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Load coverpage\ncoverpage = pd.read_csv('COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\n\n# Search for fund (case-insensitive)\nfund_name = \"Berkshire Hathaway\"\nmatches = coverpage[coverpage['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].str.contains(\n    fund_name, case=False, na=False)]\n\naccession_number = matches.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n```\n\n### Get Fund's Total AUM\n```python\n# Load summary page\nsummary = pd.read_csv('SUMMARYPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\n\n# Get AUM for specific accession number\naum_row = summary[summary['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\naum = aum_row['TABLEVALUETOTAL'].iloc[0]  # in dollars\n```\n\n### Get All Holdings for a Fund\n```python\n# Load infotable\ninfotable = pd.read_csv('INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\n\n# Get holdings\nholdings = infotable[infotable['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n\n# Count of stocks\nnum_stocks = len(holdings)\n\n# Top holdings by value\ntop_holdings = holdings.nlargest(10, 'VALUE')[['NAMEOFISSUER', 'CUSIP', 'VALUE']]\n```\n\n### Find Holdings for a Specific Stock (by CUSIP)\n```python\n# Find all funds holding Apple (CUSIP: 037833100)\napple_cusip = \"037833100\"\nfunds_holding_apple = infotable[infotable['CUSIP'] == apple_cusip]\n\n# Sort by value to find top holders\ntop_holders = funds_holding_apple.nlargest(10, 'VALUE')\n```\n\n### Compare Holdings Between Quarters\n```python\n# Load Q2 and Q3 data\nq2_coverpage = pd.read_csv('2025-q2/COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\nq3_coverpage = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\n\nq2_infotable = pd.read_csv('2025-q2/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\nq3_infotable = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\n\n# Find fund in both quarters\nfund_name = \"Berkshire Hathaway\"\n\nq2_match = q2_coverpage[q2_coverpage['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].str.contains(\n    fund_name, case=False, na=False)]\nq2_accession = q2_match.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n\nq3_match = q3_coverpage[q3_coverpage['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].str.contains(\n    fund_name, case=False, na=False)]\nq3_accession = q3_match.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n\n# Get holdings for both quarters\nq2_holdings = q2_infotable[q2_infotable['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q2_accession]\nq3_holdings = q3_infotable[q3_infotable['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q3_accession]\n\n# Merge on CUSIP to compare\ncomparison = pd.merge(\n    q2_holdings[['CUSIP', 'NAMEOFISSUER', 'VALUE']],\n    q3_holdings[['CUSIP', 'VALUE']],\n    on='CUSIP',\n    how='outer',\n    suffixes=('_q2', '_q3')\n)\n\n# Fill missing values with 0 (new positions or sold positions)\ncomparison = comparison.fillna(0)\n\n# Calculate change\ncomparison['value_change'] = comparison['VALUE_q3'] - comparison['VALUE_q2']\n\n# Find biggest increases\nbiggest_increases = comparison.nlargest(10, 'value_change')\n```\n\n## Important Notes\n\n### Value Reporting\n- **Starting January 3, 2023**: Values reported in exact dollars\n- **Before 2023**: Values reported in thousands of dollars\n- For 2025 data, use values as-is (already in dollars)\n\n### Accession Numbers\n- Format: `XXXXXXXXXX-XX-XXXXXX`\n- Changes every quarter for the same fund\n- Must search by fund name each quarter to get current accession number\n\n### CUSIP Codes\n- 9-character alphanumeric identifiers\n- Uniquely identify securities\n- Same CUSIP across all filings for the same security\n- First 6 characters identify the issuer\n- Characters 7-8 identify the specific issue\n- Character 9 is a check digit\n\n### Amendments\n- Check `ISAMENDMENT` field in COVERPAGE\n- Amended filings have `SUBMISSIONTYPE` like \"13F-HR/A\"\n- May want to use only non-amended or latest amended version\n\n### Missing Data\n- Some fields may be empty (represented as empty strings)\n- Use `na=False` in pandas `.str.contains()` to handle NaN values\n- Use `.fillna(0)` when comparing quarters to handle new/sold positions\n\n## Data Quality Checks\n\n```python\n# Check for duplicates\nduplicates = coverpage[coverpage.duplicated(subset=['ACCESSION_NUMBER'])]\n\n# Check for missing critical fields\nmissing_names = coverpage[coverpage['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].isna()]\n\n# Verify VALUE is numeric\ninfotable['VALUE'] = pd.to_numeric(infotable['VALUE'], errors='coerce')\n\n# Check for reasonable value ranges\nsuspicious = infotable[(infotable['VALUE'] < 0) | (infotable['VALUE'] > 1e12)]\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2250,"cache_read_input_tokens":31651,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2250,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1497e173-3c06-46d1-8083-5956d40a9730","uuid":"1ae6fec9-a12b-476a-81ee-9b3f5c079b23"}

Get all unique fund names\n    fund_names = coverpage_df['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].dropna().unique().tolist()\n\n    # Find best match using token_set_ratio for better matching\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        fund_name,\n        fund_names,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_set_ratio,\n        score_cutoff=threshold\n    )\n\n    if result:\n        matched_name, score = result\n        print(f\"  Matched to: '{matched_name}' (score: {score})\")\n\n        # Get all unique fund names\n    fund_names = coverpage_df['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].dropna().unique().tolist()\n\n    # Find best match using token_set_ratio for better matching\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        fund_name,\n        fund_names,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_set_ratio,\n        score_cutoff=threshold\n    )\n\n    if result:\n        matched_name, score = result\n        print(f\"  Matched to: '{matched_name}' (score: {score})\")\n\n        # Get accession number for this fund\n        match_row = coverpage_df[coverpage_df['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'] == matched_name]\n\n        if not match_row.empty:\n            accession = match_row.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n            print(f\"  Accession number: {accession}\")\n            return accession, matched_name, score\n\n    print(f\"  No match found!\")\n    return None, None, 0\n\ndef get_aum(summary_df, accession_number):\n    \"\"\"Get Assets Under Management (AUM) for a fund\"\"\"\n    row = summary_df[summary_df['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n    if not row.empty:\n        aum = row['TABLEVALUETOTAL'].iloc[0]\n        print(f\"  AUM: ${aum:,.0f}\")\n        return int(aum)\n    return None\n\ndef count_holdings(infotable_df, accession_number):\n    \"\"\"Count number of stock holdings for a fund\"\"\"\n    holdings = infotable_df[infotable_df['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n    count = len(holdings)\n    print(f\"  Number of holdings: {count}\")\n    return count\n\ndef find_cusip_by_company(infotable_df, company_name, threshold=70):\n    \"\"\"Find CUSIP for a company by fuzzy matching name\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for company '{company_name}'...\")\n\n    # Get unique company names\n    companies = infotable_df['NAMEOFISSUER'].dropna().unique().tolist()\n\n    # Find best match\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        company_name,\n        companies,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_set_ratio,\n        score_cutoff=threshold\n    )\n\n    if result:\n        matched_name, score = result\n        print(f\"  Matched to: '{matched_name}' (score: {score})\")\n\n        # Get CUSIP for this company\n        match_row = infotable_df[infotable_df['NAMEOFISSUER'] == matched_name]\n\n        if not match_row.empty:\n            cusip = match_row.iloc[0]['CUSIP']\n            print(f\"  CUSIP: {cusip}\")\n            return cusip, matched_name, score\n\n    print(f\"  No match found!\")\n    return None, None, 0\n\ndef compare_holdings(q2_infotable, q3_infotable, q2_accession, q3_accession):\n    \"\"\"Compare holdings between Q2 and Q3 to find changes\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nComparing holdings...\")\n    print(f\"  Q2 accession: {q2_accession}\")\n    print(f\"  Q3 accession: {q3_accession}\")\n\n    # Get holdings for both quarters\n    q2_holdings = q2_infotable[q2_infotable['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q2_accession].copy()\n    q3_holdings = q3_infotable[q3_infotable['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q3_accession].copy()\n\n    print(f\"  Q2 holdings: {len(q2_holdings)}\")\n    print(f\"  Q3 holdings: {len(q3_holdings)}\")\n\n    # Ensure VALUE is numeric\n    q2_holdings['VALUE'] = pd.to_numeric(q2_holdings['VALUE'], errors='coerce').fillna(0)\n    q3_holdings['VALUE'] = pd.to_numeric(q3_holdings['VALUE'], errors='coerce').fillna(0)\n\n    # Merge on CUSIP to compare\n    comparison = pd.merge(\n        q2_holdings[['CUSIP', 'NAMEOFISSUER', 'VALUE']],\n        q3_holdings[['CUSIP', 'VALUE']],\n        on='CUSIP',\n        how='outer',\n        suffixes=('_q2', '_q3')\n    )\n\n    # Fill missing values with 0 (new positions or sold positions)\n    comparison['VALUE_q2'] = comparison['VALUE_q2'].fillna(0)\n    comparison['VALUE_q3'] = comparison['VALUE_q3'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Calculate change\n    comparison['value_change'] = comparison['VALUE_q3'] - comparison['VALUE_q2']\n\n    return comparison\n\ndef find_top_holders(infotable_df, coverpage_df, cusip, top_n=3):\n    \"\"\"Find top N fund managers holding a specific stock by value\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nFinding top {top_n} holders for CUSIP {cusip}...\")\n\n    # Get all unique fund names\n    fund_names = coverpage_df['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].dropna().unique().tolist()\n\n    # Find best match using token_set_ratio for better matching\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        fund_name,\n        fund_names,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_set_ratio,\n        score_cutoff=threshold\n    )\n\n    if result:\n        matched_name, score = result\n        print(f\"  Matched to: '{matched_name}' (score: {score})\")\n\n        # Get accession number for this fund\n        match_row = coverpage_df[coverpage_df['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'] == matched_name]\n\n        if not match_row.empty:\n            accession = match_row.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n            print(f\"  Accession number: {accession}\")\n            return accession, matched_name, score\n\n    print(f\"  No match found!\")\n    return None, None, 0\n\ndef get_aum(summary_df, accession_number):\n    \"\"\"Get Assets Under Management (AUM) for a fund\"\"\"\n    row = summary_df[summary_df['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n    if not row.empty:\n        aum = row['TABLEVALUETOTAL'].iloc[0]\n        print(f\"  AUM: ${aum:,.0f}\")\n        return int(aum)\n    return None\n\ndef count_holdings(infotable_df, accession_number):\n    \"\"\"Count number of stock holdings for a fund\"\"\"\n    holdings = infotable_df[infotable_df['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n    count = len(holdings)\n    print(f\"  Number of holdings: {count}\")\n    return count\n\ndef find_cusip_by_company(infotable_df, company_name, threshold=70):\n    \"\"\"Find CUSIP for a company by fuzzy matching name\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for company '{company_name}'...\")\n\n    # Get unique company names\n    companies = infotable_df['NAMEOFISSUER'].dropna().unique().tolist()\n\n    # Find best match\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        company_name,\n        companies,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_set_ratio,\n        score_cutoff=threshold\n    )\n\n    if result:\n        matched_name, score = result\n        print(f\"  Matched to: '{matched_name}' (score: {score})\")\n\n        # Get CUSIP for this company\n        match_row = infotable_df[infotable_df['NAMEOFISSUER'] == matched_name]\n\n        if not match_row.empty:\n            cusip = match_row.iloc[0]['CUSIP']\n            print(f\"  CUSIP: {cusip}\")\n            return cusip, matched_name, score\n\n    print(f\"  No match found!\")\n    return None, None, 0\n\ndef compare_holdings(q2_infotable, q3_infotable, q2_accession, q3_accession):\n    \"\"\"Compare holdings between Q2 and Q3 to find changes\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nComparing holdings...\")\n    print(f\"  Q2 accession: {q2_accession}\")\n    print(f\"  Q3 accession: {q3_accession}\")\n\n    # Get holdings for both quarters\n    q2_holdings = q2_infotable[q2_infotable['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q2_accession].copy()\n    q3_holdings = q3_infotable[q3_infotable['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q3_accession].copy()\n\n    print(f\"  Q2 holdings: {len(q2_holdings)}\")\n    print(f\"  Q3 holdings: {len(q3_holdings)}\")\n\n    # Ensure VALUE is numeric\n    q2_holdings['VALUE'] = pd.to_numeric(q2_holdings['VALUE'], errors='coerce').fillna(0)\n    q3_holdings['VALUE'] = pd.to_numeric(q3_holdings['VALUE'], errors='coerce').fillna(0)\n\n    # Merge on CUSIP to compare\n    comparison = pd.merge(\n        q2_holdings[['CUSIP', 'NAMEOFISSUER', 'VALUE']],\n        q3_holdings[['CUSIP', 'VALUE']],\n        on='CUSIP',\n        how='outer',\n        suffixes=('_q2', '_q3')\n    )\n\n    # Fill missing values with 0 (new positions or sold positions)\n    comparison['VALUE_q2'] = comparison['VALUE_q2'].fillna(0)\n    comparison['VALUE_q3'] = comparison['VALUE_q3'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Calculate change\n    comparison['value_change'] = comparison['VALUE_q3'] - comparison['VALUE_q2']\n\n    return comparison\n\ndef find_top_holders(infotable_df, coverpage_df, cusip, top_n=3):\n    \"\"\"Find top N fund managers holding a specific stock by value\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nFinding top {top_n} holders for CUSIP {cusip}...\")\n\n    # Get all holdings for this CUSIP\n    holdings = infotable_df[infotable_df['CUSIP'] == cusip].copy()\n\n    print(f\"  Found {len(holdings)} funds holding this stock\")\n\n    # Ensure VALUE is numeric\n    holdings['VALUE'] = pd.to_numeric(holdings['VALUE'], errors='coerce').fillna(0)\n\n    # Get all unique fund names\n    fund_names = coverpage_df['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].dropna().unique().tolist()\n\n    # Find best match using token_set_ratio for better matching\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        fund_name,\n        fund_names,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_set_ratio,\n        score_cutoff=threshold\n    )\n\n    if result:\n        matched_name, score = result\n        print(f\"  Matched to: '{matched_name}' (score: {score})\")\n\n        # Get all accession numbers for this fund\n        match_rows = coverpage_df[coverpage_df['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'] == matched_name]\n\n        if not match_rows.empty:\n            # If prefer non-amended, filter out amendments\n            if prefer_non_amended and len(match_rows) > 1:\n                non_amended = match_rows[match_rows['ISAMENDMENT'] != 'Y']\n                if not non_amended.empty:\n                    match_rows = non_amended\n                    print(f\"  Using non-amended filing\")\n\n            # If still multiple, take the first (most recent in data)\n            accession = match_rows.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n            print(f\"  Accession number: {accession}\")\n            return accession, matched_name, score\n\n    print(f\"  No match found!\")\n    return None, None, 0\n\ndef get_aum(summary_df, accession_number):\n    \"\"\"Get Assets Under Management (AUM) for a fund\"\"\"\n    row = summary_df[summary_df['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n    if not row.empty:\n        aum = row['TABLEVALUETOTAL'].iloc[0]\n        print(f\"  AUM: ${aum:,.0f}\")\n        return int(aum)\n    return None\n\ndef count_holdings(infotable_df, accession_number):\n    \"\"\"Count number of stock holdings for a fund\"\"\"\n    holdings = infotable_df[infotable_df['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n    count = len(holdings)\n    print(f\"  Number of holdings: {count}\")\n    return count\n\ndef find_cusip_by_company(infotable_df, company_name, threshold=70):\n    \"\"\"Find CUSIP for a company by fuzzy matching name\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for company '{company_name}'...\")\n\n    # Get unique company names\n    companies = infotable_df['NAMEOFISSUER'].dropna().unique().tolist()\n\n    # Find best match\n    result = process.extractOne(\n        company_name,\n        companies,\n        scorer=fuzz.token_set_ratio,\n        score_cutoff=threshold\n    )\n\n    if result:\n        matched_name, score = result\n        print(f\"  Matched to: '{matched_name}' (score: {score})\")\n\n        # Get CUSIP for this company\n        match_row = infotable_df[infotable_df['NAMEOFISSUER'] == matched_name]\n\n        if not match_row.empty:\n            cusip = match_row.iloc[0]['CUSIP']\n            print(f\"  CUSIP: {cusip}\")\n            return cusip, matched_name, score\n\n    print(f\"  No match found!\")\n    return None, None, 0\n\ndef compare_holdings(q2_infotable, q3_infotable, q2_accession, q3_accession):\n    \"\"\"Compare holdings between Q2 and Q3 to find changes\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nComparing holdings...\")\n    print(f\"  Q2 accession: {q2_accession}\")\n    print(f\"  Q3 accession: {q3_accession}\")\n\n    # Fill missing values with 0 (new positions or sold positions)\n    comparison['VALUE_q2'] = comparison['VALUE_q2'].fillna(0)\n    comparison['VALUE_q3'] = comparison['VALUE_q3'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Calculate change\n    comparison['value_change'] = comparison['VALUE_q3'] - comparison['VALUE_q2']\n\n    return comparison\n\ndef find_top_holders(infotable_df, coverpage_df, cusip, top_n=3):\n    \"\"\"Find top N fund managers holding a specific stock by value\"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nFinding top {top_n} holders for CUSIP {cusip}...\")\n\n    #
